# Upstream intake ledger

Single source of truth for what we decided about each upstream PR and commit. The procedure is
[`docs/upstream-pr-intake.md`](../upstream-pr-intake.md); the older files in this directory are the **frozen archive**
of the 2026-10 reviews and are not edited.

## Boundaries

| Lane | Reviewed up to | Date |
|---|---|---|
| Open PRs | #4370 (every open PR above #3306, and every one updated since 2026-11-15, has a row) | 2026-11-16 |
| Merged PRs | #4370 (those merged since 2026-10-29) | 2026-11-16 |
| Closed, never merged PRs | #2852 (`closed-unmerged.md`); those closed since 2026-10-29 are triaged below | 2026-11-06 |
| Commits on `main` | `77c0a6a` (`upstream-main-commits.md`) | 2026-10-29 |
| Upstream `main` at this round | `a4676b1249` | 2026-11-16 |

**Round 1 (2026-11-02) scope.** Upstream was 3,300 PRs deep and 126 open PRs sat above the old #3306 boundary, so the
round triaged every one by path class and read the small shared-crate fixes in full. Deep reads (diff, body, tests,
fork code): #3298, #3370, #3414, #3422, #3441-#3448, #3464-#3470, #3495, #3512, #3515, #3523, #3567, #3574. The rest
are recorded `PENDING` with what is missing — mostly upstream-owned UI/perf/AI work whose shared files are plumbing.
Rows marked "already in the fork" name the earlier port commit, which the ledger had not recorded.

The next round starts with the `PENDING` and `WATCH` rows below, then every PR updated since 2026-11-06. Use
`python3 tools/upstream_intake.py enumerate --since 2026-11-06`.

## Ledger

`State` is as observed at review: `open`, `closed`, `merged` or `commit`. `Decision`: `ADOPT`, `LEARN`, `SKIP`,
`PENDING`, `WATCH`. `Recheck` says what re-opens the row (`-` for a final decision). `Upstream ref` is the reviewed
PR head or commit SHA. A pipe inside a cell is escaped with a backslash.

| Item | State | Title | Decision | Recheck | Reason | Upstream ref | Reviewed | Fork |

| #3856 | merged | GPU float output for the HDR render (F32Hdr) | SKIP | - | Already in Chromaforge in its own design. Fork 3e24041c8 ("Gap 14: HDR renders on the GPU", 2026-10-21) made `handles` take F32Hdr (`crates/gpu/src/lib.rs:1092`). In `crates/gpu/src/render.rs:770-1064`, the finish kernel writes the 8-bit image plus a linear float tail (`DEEP`, `PEAK`), keeps its unwritten-pixel check and skips `req.display` for deep renders. In `finish.wgsl`, the exact sRGB curve is unbounded above 1 and curves continue above SDR white (`lut_hdr`). CPU-oracle equivalence tests come with it. Upstream's layout differs (3 words per pixel with a sign-bit `DEEP_WRITTEN` sentinel, no 8-bit plane); not worth swapping. | `f26e3e1a52` | 2026-11-09 @ f6b57b0b59 | - |
